﻿1700_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = character:﻿1700
			exists = character:145129
		}
		character:﻿1700 = { save_scope_as = awow_child}
		character:145129 = { save_scope_as = awow_other_mother}
		awow_assign_historic_father_as_other_mother = yes
		clear_saved_scope = awow_child
		clear_saved_scope = awow_other_mother
	}
}

makrembolites_3_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = character:makrembolites_3
			exists = character:makrembolites_2
		}
		character:makrembolites_3 = { save_scope_as = awow_child}
		character:makrembolites_2 = { save_scope_as = awow_other_mother}
		awow_assign_historic_father_as_other_mother = yes
		clear_saved_scope = awow_child
		clear_saved_scope = awow_other_mother
	}
}
